# Skills · S · part 73

838 entries, see to seedance-cinematic. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **see**: S.EE (SEE) platform API integration for short URL management, text sharing, and file sharing. ([jtsang4/efficient-coding](https://github.com/jtsang4/efficient-coding/tree/HEAD/skills/see) · ★2)
- **see**: Lets an agent that reads images but no video see a recording frame by frame and measure a screen to the pixel. Use it to check an animation or a transition over time, compare two versions of one, find what differs between two screenshots, or measure sizes, padding and gaps on a screenshot against a design. ([ecstra/agent-setup](https://github.com/ecstra/agent-setup/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/see) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **see**: repo-brief Clone a GitHub repository to ~/ai/ and generate a structured technical brief for understanding how the project is implemented — architecture, core mechanisms, design decisions, failure modes, and a guided comprehension path. Use when the user provides a GitHub repo URL and asks for analysis, a brief, a report, or wants to understand a project's implementation principles. ([luw2007/skills](https://github.com/luw2007/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/see) · ★1)
- **see**: Debug from a screenshot or a short screen recording - Gemini watches it next to your source code and points to the components and lines responsible. ([vmishra/gemini-sidecar](https://github.com/vmishra/gemini-sidecar/tree/HEAD/plugins/gemini/skills/see) · ★1)
- **see**: Inspect rendered visual output by capturing and viewing screenshots of it yourself. Use after changing a web page, HTML document, UI, canvas, or visual asset, or when asked whether something is readable, laid out correctly, or visually rough. Launching the app is the run skill's job; building a chart is the dataviz skill's. ([overnightworks/marketplace](https://github.com/overnightworks/marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/atelier/skills/see))
- **see**: Capture the screen and inject a short text summary into Claude's context via an in-process VLM (llama-cpp-python). No daemon. Use to feed terminal/editor/browser state to Claude for debug, fix suggestions, or summarization with minimal token cost. ([qte77/cc-senses-plugin](https://github.com/qte77/cc-senses-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/see))

- **see-also**: Generate Wikipedia-style See Also sections — austere lists of kin objects, concepts, texts, practices, or phenomena adjacent to a given subject without arguing or explaining the kinship. The reader performs the binding. Default deployment is reflexive: produce the See Also for *this conversation* — what would appear at the bottom of the Wikipedia article our chat would constitute. Form rules: bullet list, 5-15 entries, no editorial gloss (brief disambiguating parenthetical only when truly needed), no thesis, mix of obvious and slightly-oblique entries. The slightly-strange entry is load-beari… ([Wondermonger-daydreaming/claude-skills-library](https://github.com/Wondermonger-daydreaming/claude-skills-library/tree/HEAD/skills/see-also) · ★7)

- **see-it**: Turn text, code, a PR or diff, a design doc, config, or anything else into layered Mermaid diagrams, from an overview down to detail, each readable at a glance. Diagrams only, no prose. Use when the user says "see it", "diagram this", "draw this", "visualize this", "help me understand this", or "help me understand this diff". ([knwoop/see-it](https://github.com/knwoop/see-it/tree/HEAD/skills/see-it) · ★2)
- **see-it**: Let a reviewer see and try a change before it merges, as a private page built from the PR's own packages. Use when a PR (or a release PR) changes anything a person sees or touches, when you say work is ready, or when a design question is easier to show than to describe. ([marktiderman/genesis](https://github.com/marktiderman/genesis/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/see-it))
- **see-it-first**: Forces reproduction before any fix. When something doesn't work, this skill stops the symptom→guess→patch loop and first reproduces the bug locally on the same branch, data, and inputs the user saw — because an unreproduced bug is an unsolved one. 버그·이상 동작 앞에서 추측 패치의 유혹을 끊고, 먼저 눈앞에 똑같이 재현하게 만드는 엔지니어링 철학 스킬. ([kwakseongjae/some-engineer-mind](https://github.com/kwakseongjae/some-engineer-mind/tree/HEAD/see-it-first) · ★1)
- **see-it-in-figma**: Prototype a design problem in Figma against Cornerstone's real variables and components, before anyone commits to an implementation. Use when someone wants to see, sketch, mock up, try, or compare an interface idea — a state that has no component yet, an empty or error state, a layout or density question, a variant that may not be needed — or wants to check whether an idea holds up across Cru and FamilyLife in light and dark. Figma only — it never writes application code. ([CruGlobal/cornerstone-design-system](https://github.com/CruGlobal/cornerstone-design-system/tree/HEAD/plugins/cornerstone-designer/skills/see-it-in-figma) · ★1)
- **see-it-live**: Launch the app and confirm the specific change is visible and working: screenshot, curl, or run it. Doubles as the fast smoke pass. Use for run the app, start the server, see it live, smoke test, or post-deploy check. ([alex-macra/claude-codex-skills-assembly](https://github.com/alex-macra/claude-codex-skills-assembly/tree/HEAD/skills/see-it-live) · ★1)
- **see-it-through**: Use when the user wants Codex or Claude Code to persist a detailed checkpoint plan first, keep updating it honestly, and continue until every checkpoint is finished, paused, blocked, or cancelled. ([Olorinm/see-it-through](https://github.com/Olorinm/see-it-through/tree/HEAD/plugins/see-it-through/skills/see-it-through) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **see-it-work**: Prove a change to an Expo app or a website works by seeing it, not by trusting a passing test. Picks which layer proves this change (component test, seeded simulator screenshot, smoke case, release build smoke run, browser preview, end to end journey), runs it, and shows the evidence. Use after any UI, navigation, layout, purchase or deep link change, before saying something is fixed or done, and when setting up testing in a new app. ([namedtoaster/The-Last-Mile](https://github.com/namedtoaster/The-Last-Mile/tree/HEAD/skills/see-it-work) · ★39)
- **see-luft-bahn-multimodal-bearbeiten**: Bestimme Verkehrsträger, Teilstrecke, Schadensort und anwendbares Haftungsregime; die internationalen Übereinkommen nicht austauschbar behandeln. Die zugeordneten Fachmodule und Vorlagen werden nach dem konkreten Auftrag gelesen und zusammengeführt. ([Klotzkette/claude-fuer-deutsches-recht](https://github.com/Klotzkette/claude-fuer-deutsches-recht/tree/HEAD/fachanwalt-transport-speditionsrecht/skills/see-luft-bahn-multimodal-bearbeiten) · ★1.9k)
- **see-lunte**: See Lunte's UI to check a change, since the screen itself can't be captured from here. Renders every popover, onboarding, Club and Settings state to PNG, snapshots the real menu bar panel with real data, installs the build on this Mac, and samples colours against the user's screenshots. Use before and after any change to the popover, panel, Club window, Settings or onboarding, and whenever the user says "install it" or "does it look right". ([ProggePal/lunte-app](https://github.com/ProggePal/lunte-app/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/see-lunte) · ★2)
- **see-pattern**: 跨关系模式觉察 + 成长叙事。合并原 pattern-mirror + growth-story。当用户积累足够历史线索后，帮用户看到跨关系的重复行为模式，并在有变化数据时呈现成长叙事。历史材料优先来自 `MEMORY.md` 与 Slow guidance。心理学基础：叙事疗法重写（Michael White）、CBT 模式识别、IFS 部分工作（Richard Schwartz）、创新时刻理论（Innovative Moments）。 ([yuyuxinli/moodcoco](https://github.com/yuyuxinli/moodcoco/tree/HEAD/backend/skills/see-pattern) · ★1)
- **see-surf-ssrf**: See-SURF: crawling plus SSRF dual probing. Signals: web vulnerability, injection, crawl-based SSRF detection, OOB callback, concurrency. ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/tool-skills/see-surf-ssrf))

- **see-the-dashboard**: See where the product stands right now, composed on demand from several read models and never committed. Teaches why a dashboard is the clearest case the publication test rejects, and why a document may compose many views while each view keeps exactly one owner. Ask at any moment. ([nuewframe/praxis](https://github.com/nuewframe/praxis/tree/HEAD/skills/see-the-dashboard))
- **see-through**: Keeps 2D characters visible behind trees, fog, miasma, walls, and roofs using four context-routed looks: Fade (A: single tree), 4x4 Bayer Dither (B: fog/mist/canopy zones), Hard Cutout (C: spell spheres/miasma/energy zones), and Clipped Silhouette with Stealth Alarm-Red state (D: walls/stealth near discovery). Includes pre-code project audit, depth + 3x4 opaque cover testing, 0.2s per-object easing, Godot 4 / Unity scripts & shaders, and 10-point automated verification (`tools/see_through.py`). ([mulosbron/game-dev-skills](https://github.com/mulosbron/game-dev-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/see-through))
